# Material Symbols Rounded (`msr`) — 3004 icons

- **Prefix:** `Msr` · **Version:** 0.9.0 · **License:** Apache-2.0 · **Style:** fill · **viewBox:** `0 -960 960 960`
- **Source:** [marella/material-symbols](https://github.com/marella/material-symbols)
- Google's Material Symbols — the rounded weight-400 variant (e.g. `MsrHome`, `MsrSearch`, `MsrFace`). 960×960 viewBox; paths paint with `currentColor`; theme via `color`.

Import default-only per icon: `import MsrExample from "@mal-icons/react/msr/MsrExample"`, or named from the set barrel: `import { MsrExample } from "@mal-icons/react/msr"`.
